Executable triage was unavailable for this entire reply because eic.self_employed and eic.clergy fail type validation (`triage/eic.deepseek.json`, state invalid), and eic.credit also declares eic.self_employed and eic.clergy in its refuses list. By reading, it omits the Step 1 question 1 AGI limit, Step 1 question 2 filer EIC SSN test, Step 2 investment income test, and Worksheet B, computing the Worksheet A credit without those STOPs. No sampled equivalence is claimed.

Ruling: model error — i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A, line 6. Executable triage was unavailable for this entire reply because eic.self_employed and eic.clergy fail validation (`triage/eic.deepseek.json`, state invalid), and eic.line6 lists eic.clergy in refuses. By reading, it omits shown_when: eic.eligible and computes line 6 after earlier STOPs from a line 1 that ignores Worksheet B. No sampled equivalence is claimed.

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eic.credit | eic.clergy, eic.self_employed, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.clergy, eic.self_employed |
| eic.check_27c | eic.clergy, eic.self_employed, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.clergy, eic.self_employed |
| (Step 5, 1) | eic.clergy | eic.clergy |
| (Step 5, 2) | scope.other_income | - |
| (Step 5, 3) | eic.clergy, scope.other_income | eic.clergy |
| (Step 5, 4) | s8812.combat_pay | - |
| (Step 5, 5) | eic.clergy, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.clergy |
| 4b | eic.clergy, eic.self_employed, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.clergy, eic.self_employed |
| 6 (eic.wsb_6) | eic.clergy, eic.self_employed, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.clergy, eic.self_employed |
| 1 | eic.clergy, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.clergy |
| 2 | eic.clergy, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.clergy |
| 4 | eic.clergy, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.clergy |
| 6 (eic.line6) | eic.clergy, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.clergy |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    refuses: [eic.clergy]
    note: "The clergy/church-employee adjustment to line 1 is not supplied by the input interface; eic.clergy refuses this line when the supplied Schedule SE screen is true."
  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "No Medicaid waiver payment amount is supplied, so this is zero when the supplied other-income screen is false."
  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.step5_line1, take: eic.step5_line2}
  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]
    note: "No nontaxable combat pay amount or election is supplied, so this is zero when the combat-pay screen is false."
  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.step5_line3, eic.step5_line4]}
  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.earned_income}
    refuses: [eic.self_employed]
    note: "Worksheet B Parts 1 through 3 amounts are not in the supplied interface. When eic.self_employed is false those parts are zero and line 4b is the Step 5 earned income; when true this line refuses."
  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: eic.wsb_4b, take: {param: ss.zero}}
    note: "If line 4b is zero or less the instructions stop; this line floors that case to zero."
  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.earned_income}
  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.child_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_credit3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.child_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_credit2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_credit1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_credit0
  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}
  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}
  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "If you have no qualifying children who have valid SSNs, is line 3 less than the applicable threshold? If one or more qualifying children, is line 3 less than the applicable threshold? If yes, leave line 5 blank; if no, look up line 3 in the EIC Table and enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_threshold]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.line3, eic.h_threshold]}
          then: eic.h_line3_credit
  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - op: test
                not:
                  op: test
                  eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]
              - op: test
                eq: [eic.h_below_threshold, {const: true}]
          then: eic.line2
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - op: test
                not:
                  op: test
                  eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]
              - op: test
                not:
                  op: test
                  eq: [eic.h_below_threshold, {const: true}]
          then: eic.h_smaller_5_2
  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.has_sc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.clergy, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ineligible, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ineligible, {const: true}]}}
          then: eic.line6
    refuses: [eic.self_employed]
  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on line 27c if you do not want to claim the earned income credit or if instructed to check the box."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ineligible, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.line6, {const: 0}]}
    refuses: [eic.self_employed]
  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [eic.child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              not:
                op: test
                eq: [eic.same_home, {const: true}]
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: true}]}
    note: "Read as the separated-spouse special-rule box: married filing separately or head of household, at least one EIC qualifying child with a valid SSN, and either not the same principal residence for the last 6 months or legally separated and living apart at year end."
  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income of $108.28 or more?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.se_screen}
    note: "The supplied interface has no direct clergy/church-employee question, so this uses person.se_screen, the supplied gate for unsupported Schedule SE situations including clergy and church employee income."
  - id: eic.child_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs for the EIC."
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn_child, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_thr0j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No qualifying children, married filing jointly threshold."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}
  - id: eic.h_thr0o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No qualifying children, other filing status threshold."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.other}
  - id: eic.h_thr1j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One or more qualifying children, married filing jointly threshold."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}
  - id: eic.h_thr1o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One or more qualifying children, other filing status threshold."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.other}
  - id: eic.h_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Applicable line 5 threshold."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
          then: eic.h_thr0j
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
              - op: test
                not:
                  op: test
                  eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]
          then: eic.h_thr0o
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, ne: [eic.child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
          then: eic.h_thr1j
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, ne: [eic.child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
              - op: test
                not:
                  op: test
                  eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]
          then: eic.h_thr1o
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: eic.h_credit0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit with no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_credit1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit with one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_credit2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit with two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_credit3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit with three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line3_credit0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3 with no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line3_credit1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3 with one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line3_credit2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3 with two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line3_credit3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3 with three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line3_credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3 by number of qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.child_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line3_credit3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.child_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line3_credit2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line3_credit1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line3_credit0
  - id: eic.h_below_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 is less than the applicable line 5 threshold."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_threshold]}
  - id: eic.h_smaller_5_2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 5 and line 2."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}
  - id: eic.h_ineligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A screening condition requires the credit to stop at zero or to check line 27c."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          not:
            op: test
            eq: [household.all_eic_ssn, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
            - op: test
              not:
                op: test
                eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]
```
